- **crab-architect**: Compares the architecture sections of the prey and maw digests and proposes at most three structural nutrients with evidence. Use for architecture candidates from crab compare, or when asked how another repository is structured. ([drevendev/HungryCrab](https://github.com/drevendev/HungryCrab/blob/HEAD/agents/crab-architect.md) · ★5)
- **crab-cleanroom-impl**: Implements a REIMPLEMENT nutrient inside the maw from a code-free clean-room specification, without access to prey source or the Hungry Crab cache. Use only after the cleanroom skill has produced the specification at the path `crab spec &lt;nutrient-id&gt;` prints, under `.crab/specs/`. ([drevendev/HungryCrab](https://github.com/drevendev/HungryCrab/blob/HEAD/agents/crab-cleanroom-impl.md) · ★5)
- **crab-historian**: Turns the history and branches sections of a prey digest into 3-7 concrete lessons with evidence. ([drevendev/HungryCrab](https://github.com/drevendev/HungryCrab/blob/HEAD/agents/crab-historian.md) · ★5)
- **crabcode-security**: The dedicated CrabCode Security orchestrator. Hand it an unattended job — "fully scan this repository and patch what you find; I understand it will use a lot of tokens" — and it runs the whole thing itself: capturing the revision, driving the multi-agent scan through the crabcode-security:scan workflow, assembling the verified report, and turning survivors into targeted patch files you apply when you choose, each verified by a panel of agents before it is written. Best as the main agent of a session. ([acosmi/CrabCode-Plugin](https://github.com/acosmi/CrabCode-Plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/crabcode-security/agents/crabcode-security.md) · ★1)
- **cracked-coder**: Implements complex features, fixes bugs, and optimizes performance using a strict TDD workflow. - **crash-triage**: Read-only. It takes a ScarletNX crash report (or svcBreak, or the `threads.txt` of a hang). It walks the disassembly of the game back from the instruction that faulted to the origin of the bad value. It reports the mechanism with the addresses that it read. Give it the log path (or the crash section), the extracted game folder (`work/&lt;Game&gt;`), and anything that you already know. Use it to keep large disassembly out of the main context. ([scarletnx/ScarletNX](https://github.com/scarletnx/ScarletNX/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/crash-triage.md) · ★350 · 2 copies)

- **crewai-agent-to-agent-handoff-and-interaction-api**: CrewAI provides two distinct handoff mechanisms — sequential task chaining via the context parameter and hierarchical delegation via a manager agent — and the choice between them determines whether agent interaction is data-driven (outputs flow as context) or role-driven (manager assigns work dynamically at runtime).
